            local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                return
            end

            if not getgenv().deselectedplayer or getgenv().deselectedplayer == "No Cars Found" then
                return
            end

            local HumanoidRootPartPosition = HumanoidRootPart.Position
            local Anchored = HumanoidRootPart.Anchored
            local Vehicles = workspace:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

            if not Vehicles then
                return
            end

            local v1701 = Vehicles:FindFirstChild(getgenv().deselectedplayer .. "Car")

            if not v1701 then
                return
            end

            local VehicleSeat = v1701:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat")

            if not VehicleSeat then
                local GetDescendants = v1701.GetDescendants

                for _, v in ipairs(GetDescendants(v1701)) do
                    if v:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
                        VehicleSeat = v

                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if not VehicleSeat then
                return
            end

            local Occupant = VehicleSeat.Occupant

            if Occupant then
                local OccupantParent = Occupant.Parent
                local v1708 = OccupantParent and t1[5]:GetPlayerFromCharacter(OccupantParent)
                local v1709 = v1708 and v1708.Name or (OccupantParent and OccupantParent.Name or "Unknown")
                local u1710 = v1709

                    local v1898 = t1[18]

                    local v1903 = v1900("Player " .. str .. " is seated on this vehicle")

                    MakeNotification(v1898, {
							Name = v1899,
							Image = "rbxassetid://10709752996",
							Content = v1903,
							Time = 5
						})
                end)

                return
            end

            Character:PivotTo(VehicleSeat.CFrame)
            task.wait(0.1)

            local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if Humanoid then
                local timestamp = tick()

                HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false

                while tick() - timestamp < 5 and VehicleSeat ~= Humanoid.SeatPart do
                    HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                    Humanoid.Sit = true
                    pcall(firetouchinterest, HumanoidRootPart, VehicleSeat, 0)
                    pcall(firetouchinterest, HumanoidRootPart, VehicleSeat, 1)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end

                if VehicleSeat ~= Humanoid.SeatPart then
                    HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPartPosition)
                    HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = Anchored

                    return
                end

                task.wait(0.5)

                local vector3 = Vector3.new(9999999999, 999999999, 9999999999)

                VehicleSeat.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector3)
                task.wait(0.3)

                if VehicleSeat == Humanoid.SeatPart then
                    pcall(function()
                        Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                    end)
                    Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end

                pcall(function()
                    Humanoid:LeaveVehicle()
                end)
                task.wait(0.5)

                local vector3_4 = Vector3.new(HumanoidRootPartPosition.X, HumanoidRootPartPosition.Y + 50, HumanoidRootPartPosition.Z)

                Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(vector3_4))
                task.wait(0.2)
                Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPartPosition))
                HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = Anchored
            end
        end)

            if not t1[2].humanoid or not t1[2].part then
                return
            end
            t1[2].ctrl = t1[2].ctrl or {
				f = 0,
				b = 0,
				l = 0,
				r = 0
			}
            t1[2].flying = true
            t1[2].humanoid.PlatformStand = true
            local v1021 = t1[2].flightSpeed or 50
            local n11 = 0
            t1[2].bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro", t1[2].part)
            t1[2].bg.P = 90000
            t1[2].bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
            t1[2].bg.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
            t1[2].bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", t1[2].part)
            t1[2].bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
            t1[2].bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
            if not t1[2].UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
                if t1[2].inputConnBegan then
                    t1[2].inputConnBegan:Disconnect()
                    t1[2].inputConnBegan = nil
                end

                if t1[2].inputConnEnded then
                    t1[2].inputConnEnded:Disconnect()
                    t1[2].inputConnEnded = nil
                end

                t1[2].inputConnBegan = t1[2].UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                    if gameProcessed then
                        return
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
                        t1[2].ctrl.f = -1
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
                        t1[2].ctrl.b = 1
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
                        t1[2].ctrl.l = -1
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
                        t1[2].ctrl.r = 1
                    end
                end)
                t1[2].inputConnEnded = t1[2].UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                    if gameProcessed then
                        return
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
                        t1[2].ctrl.f = 0
                        t1[2].ctrl.b = 0
                    end

                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
                        t1[2].ctrl.l = 0
                        t1[2].ctrl.r = 0
                    end
                end)
            end
            if t1[2].renderConn then
                t1[2].renderConn:Disconnect()
                t1[2].renderConn = nil
            end
            t1[2].renderConn = t1[2].RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(_)
                local flying = t1[2].flying

                if flying then
                    flying = t1[2].bg

                    if flying then
                        flying = t1[2].bv and (t1[2].part and t1[2].humanoid)
                    end
                end

                if not flying then
                    return
                end

                local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

                if not CurrentCamera then
                    return
                end

                if not t1[2].UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
                    if t1[2].ctrl.l + t1[2].ctrl.r ~= 0 or t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b ~= 0 then
                        n11 = n11 + 0.5 + n11 / v1021

                        if n11 > v1021 then
                            n11 = v1021
                        end
                    elseif n11 ~= 0 then
                        n11 -= 1

                        if n11 < 0 then
                            n11 = 0
                        end
                    end

                    local CurrentCameraCFrame = CurrentCamera.CFrame
                    local v1740 = (CurrentCameraCFrame.LookVector * (t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b) + ((CurrentCameraCFrame * CFrame.new(t1[2].ctrl.l + t1[2].ctrl.r, (t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b) * 0.2, 0)).p - CurrentCameraCFrame.p)) * n11

                    if (t1[2].ctrl.l + t1[2].ctrl.r == 0 or t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b ~= 0) and n11 ~= 0 then
                        v1740 = (CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 0 + (CurrentCamera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0).p - CurrentCamera.CFrame.p)) * n11
                    elseif (t1[2].ctrl.l + t1[2].ctrl.r == 0 or t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b ~= 0) and n11 == 0 then
                        v1740 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    end

                    if t1[2].ctrl.l + t1[2].ctrl.r ~= 0 or t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b ~= 0 then
                        local _ = t1[2].ctrl.f
                        local _ = t1[2].ctrl.b
                        local _ = t1[2].ctrl.l
                        local _ = t1[2].ctrl.r
                    end

                    t1[2].bv.Velocity = v1740
                    t1[2].bg.CFrame = CurrentCamera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(-math.rad((t1[2].ctrl.f + t1[2].ctrl.b) * 50 * (n11 / v1021)), 0, 0)

                    return
                end

                local MoveDirection = t1[2].humanoid.MoveDirection
                local vector3 = Vector3.new(MoveDirection.X, 0, MoveDirection.Z)
                local vector3_5 = Vector3.new(CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
                local vector3_6 = Vector3.new(CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.Z)
                local n12 = 0
                local n13 = 0

                if vector3.Magnitude > 0 then
                    n13 = vector3:Dot(vector3_5.Unit)
                    n12 = vector3:Dot(vector3_6.Unit)
                end

                local v1751 = CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * n13 + CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector * n12
                local v1752 = if not (v1751.Magnitude > 0) then Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) else v1751.Unit * math.clamp(MoveDirection.Magnitude * t1[2].flightSpeed or v1021, 0, v1021)

                t1[2].bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(v1752.X, v1752.Y, v1752.Z)
                t1[2].bg.CFrame = CurrentCamera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(-math.rad(n13 * 30), 0, 0)
            end)
        end

                local v1040 = t1[2].turboActive and t1[2].turboAcceleration or t1[2].acceleration

                    t1[2].speed = math.clamp(t1[2].speed + v1040, -v1039, v1039)

                end
                v1852(p178.Character or p178.CharacterAdded:Wait())
                p178.CharacterAdded:Connect(v1852)
                self.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not _G.AntiFling then
                        return

                        end

                            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                descendant.CanCollide = false

Tab:AddToggle({
	Name = t1[10]("Anti Doors Fling"),
	Default = false,
	PremiumOnly = true,
	Flag = "AntiDoorsFling",
	Callback = function(p183)
    if not _G.hiddenDoors then
        _G.hiddenDoors = {}
    end

    if p183 then
        _G.hiddenDoors = {}

        for _, descendant in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name:lower():find("door") then
                local descendantTransparency = descendant.Transparency
                local CanCollide = descendant.CanCollide
                local CastShadow = descendant.CastShadow
                local t191 = {
						door = descendant,
						originalTransparency = descendantTransparency,
						originalCanCollide = CanCollide,
						originalCastShadow = CastShadow
					}

                descendant.Transparency = 1
                descendant.CanCollide = false
                descendant.CastShadow = false

                local GetChildren = descendant.GetChildren

                for _, v in ipairs(GetChildren(descendant)) do
                    if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                        v.Transparency = 1
                        v.CanCollide = false
                    elseif v:IsA("SurfaceGui") or v:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        v.Enabled = false
                    end
                end

                table.insert(_G.hiddenDoors, t191)
            end
        end

        return
    end

    for _, v in ipairs(_G.hiddenDoors or {}) do
        if v.door and v.door.Parent then
            v.door.Transparency = v.originalTransparency
            v.door.CanCollide = v.originalCanCollide
            v.door.CastShadow = v.originalCastShadow

            for _, child in ipairs(v.door:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                    child.Transparency = 0
                    child.CanCollide = true
                elseif child:IsA("SurfaceGui") or child:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                    child.Enabled = true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    _G.hiddenDoors = {}
end
})

t1[128] = game.PlaceId
t1[129] = {}
t1[130] = ""
t1[131] = os.date("!*t").hour
function TPReturner()
    local v1302 = if t1[130] ~= "" then game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. t1[128] .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor=" .. t1[130])) else game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. t1[128] .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
    local s2 = ""

    if v1302.nextPageCursor and (v1302.nextPageCursor ~= "null" and v1302.nextPageCursor ~= nil) then
        t1[130] = v1302.nextPageCursor
    end

    local n24 = 0

    for _, v in pairs(v1302.data) do
        local v1307 = true

        s2 = tostring(v.id)

        if tonumber(v.maxPlayers) > tonumber(v.playing) then

            for v1310, v1311 in pairs(t1[129]) do

                if n24 ~= 0 then
                    if s2 == tostring(v1311) then
                        v1307 = false
                    end
                elseif tonumber(t1[131]) ~= tonumber(v1311) then
                    pcall(function()
                        t1[129] = {}
                        table.insert(t1[129], t1[131])
                    end)
                end

                n24 += 1
            end
            if v1307 == true then
                local insert = table.insert
                local v1313 = s2

                insert(t1[129], v1313)
                wait()
                pcall(function()
                    wait()
                    game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(t1[128], s2, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
                end)
                wait(4)
            end
        end
    end
end
function Teleport()
    while wait() do
        pcall(function()
            TPReturner()

            if t1[130] ~= "" then
                TPReturner()
            end
        end)
    end
end

        end)

local v299 = (function()
    if identifyexecutor then
        local ok, result = pcall(identifyexecutor)

        if ok then
            ok = type(result) == "string" and #result > 0
        end

        if ok then
            return result
        end
    end

    if syn then
        return "Synapse X"
    end

    if KRNL_LOADED then
        return "Krnl"
    end

    if is_sirhurt_closure then
        return "SirHurt"
    end

    if pebc_execute then
        return "ProtoSmasher"
    end

    if secure_load then
        return "Sentinel"
    end

    return "Unknown"
end)()

local v301 = (function()
    if gethwid then
        local ok, result = pcall(gethwid)

        if ok and result then
            return tostring(result)
        end
    end

    if syn and syn.get_hwid then
        local ok, result = pcall(syn.get_hwid)

        if ok and result then
            return tostring(result)
        end
    end

    if get_hwid then
        local ok, result = pcall(get_hwid)

        if ok and result then
            return tostring(result)
        end
    end

    return "unknown"
end)()
local Name = t1[138].Name
local UserId = t1[138].UserId
local v304 = t1[140]
local v305 = t1[141]
local JobId = game.JobId
local str = tostring(game.PlaceId)
local v308 = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

t1[143] = {
	username = Name,
	userId = UserId,
	country = v304,
	state = v305,
	game = "Unknown",
	server = JobId,
	placeId = str,
	time = v308,
	execution = true,
	executor = v299,
	script = "SP Hub Brookhaven",
	device = v300,
	hwid = v301
}
task.spawn(function()
    local json = t1[134]:JSONEncode(t1[143])
    local t193 = {
		["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
	}

    if t1[134].HttpEnabled then
        pcall(function()
            t1[134]:PostAsync(t1[139], json, Enum.HttpContentType.ApplicationJson, false, t193)
        end)
    end

    for _, v in ipairs({
		http_request,
		request,
		syn and syn.request,
		http and http.request,
		fluxus and fluxus.request
	}) do
        if v then
            pcall(function()
                v({
					Url = t1[139],
					Method = "POST",
					Headers = t193,
					Body = json
				})
            end)

            return
        end
    end
end)
